+int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    const int remainder = total % blocks;
+    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
+}
+
+int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
+// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
+// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
+int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
+    int active = 1;
+    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
+    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
+        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
+        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
+    }
+    return active;
+}
+
+// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
+// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
+    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
+    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
+    world.materials = {
+        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
+        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
+        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
+    };
+
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
+    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
+
+    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+
+    const size_t local_elements =
+        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
+    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
+    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
+
+    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
+}
+
+inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
+                                  const int global_col) {
+    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
+        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
+        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
+}
+
+inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
+                         const val_t other_energy) {
+    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
+}
+
+inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
+                          const int local_col) {
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
+    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
+    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
+    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
+    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
+
+    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
+
+    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
+    // original implementation.
+    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
+                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
+                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_row > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
+                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
+                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
+                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
+                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+
+    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
+    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
+}

+void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
+    request_count = 0;
+    const int rows = world.local_rows;
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+
+    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
+        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
+    }
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
+                  &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
 }

+                if (!is_interior) {
+                    updateElement(world, local_row, local_col);
+                }

-            
-            // Update element state
-            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
-            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
-            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
+
+        world.energy.swap(world.next_energy);
+        world.total_flux.swap(world.next_total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+// Collect a distributed field in global row-major order.  This is used only
+// for optional validation and result printing; the normal benchmark path does
+// not replicate the mesh on rank zero.
+void gatherGlobalState(const World& world, const int rank, const int process_count,
+                       const bool active, const int n_elems_root,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_energy,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_total_flux) {
+    const int local_count = active ? static_cast<int>(world.energy.size()) : 0;
+    const int local_metadata[4] = {
+        active ? world.row_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.col_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.local_rows : 0,
+        active ? world.local_cols : 0,
+    };
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<int> metadata;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(process_count);
+        displacements.resize(process_count);
+        metadata.resize(static_cast<size_t>(process_count) * 4);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&local_count, 1, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gather(local_metadata, 4, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? metadata.data() : nullptr, 4, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int gathered_count = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            displacements[process] = gathered_count;
+            gathered_count += counts[process];
+        }
     }
+
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_energy;
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_flux;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        gathered_energy.resize(gathered_count);
+        gathered_flux.resize(gathered_count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.energy.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.total_flux.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_flux.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t global_count = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
+    global_energy.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    global_total_flux.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+        const int row_begin = metadata[4 * process];
+        const int col_begin = metadata[4 * process + 1];
+        const int rows = metadata[4 * process + 2];
+        const int cols = metadata[4 * process + 3];
+        const int source_begin = displacements[process];
+        for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            const size_t destination =
+                static_cast<size_t>(row_begin + row) * n_elems_root + col_begin;
+            const size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(source_begin) +
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols;
+            std::memcpy(global_energy.data() + destination, gathered_energy.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+            std::memcpy(global_total_flux.data() + destination, gathered_flux.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+        }
+    }
 }

-// Material type IDs
-constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;

+constexpr int TAG_TOP = 100;
+constexpr int TAG_BOTTOM = 101;
+constexpr int TAG_LEFT = 102;
+constexpr int TAG_RIGHT = 103;
+
+int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    const int remainder = total % blocks;
+    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
+}
+
+int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
+// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
+// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
+int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
+    int active = 1;
+    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
+    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
+        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
+        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
+    }
+    return active;
+}
+
+// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
+// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
+    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
+    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
+    world.materials = {
+        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
+        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
+        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
+    };
+
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
+    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
+
+    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+
+    const size_t local_elements =
+        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
+    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
+    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
+
+    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
+}
+
+inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
+                                  const int global_col) {
+    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
+        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
+        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
+}
+
+inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
+                         const val_t other_energy) {
+    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
+}
+
+inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
+                          const int local_col) {
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
+    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
+    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
+    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
+    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
+
+    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
+
+    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
+    // original implementation.
+    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
+                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
+                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_row > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
+                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
+                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
+                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
+                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+
+    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
+    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
+}

-inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
-                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {

+void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
+    request_count = 0;
+    const int rows = world.local_rows;
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+
+    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
+        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
+    }
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
+                  &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
 }

+// Collect a distributed field in global row-major order.  This is used only
+// for optional validation and result printing; the normal benchmark path does
+// not replicate the mesh on rank zero.
+void gatherGlobalState(const World& world, const int rank, const int process_count,
+                       const bool active, const int n_elems_root,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_energy,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_total_flux) {
+    const int local_count = active ? static_cast<int>(world.energy.size()) : 0;
+    const int local_metadata[4] = {
+        active ? world.row_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.col_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.local_rows : 0,
+        active ? world.local_cols : 0,
+    };
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<int> metadata;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(process_count);
+        displacements.resize(process_count);
+        metadata.resize(static_cast<size_t>(process_count) * 4);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&local_count, 1, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gather(local_metadata, 4, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? metadata.data() : nullptr, 4, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int gathered_count = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            displacements[process] = gathered_count;
+            gathered_count += counts[process];
+        }
     }
+
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_energy;
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_flux;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        gathered_energy.resize(gathered_count);
+        gathered_flux.resize(gathered_count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.energy.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.total_flux.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_flux.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t global_count = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
+    global_energy.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    global_total_flux.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+        const int row_begin = metadata[4 * process];
+        const int col_begin = metadata[4 * process + 1];
+        const int rows = metadata[4 * process + 2];
+        const int cols = metadata[4 * process + 3];
+        const int source_begin = displacements[process];
+        for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            const size_t destination =
+                static_cast<size_t>(row_begin + row) * n_elems_root + col_begin;
+            const size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(source_begin) +
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols;
+            std::memcpy(global_energy.data() + destination, gathered_energy.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+            std::memcpy(global_total_flux.data() + destination, gathered_flux.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+        }
+    }
 }

+constexpr int TAG_TOP = 100;

+int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    const int remainder = total % blocks;
+    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
+}
+
+int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
+// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
+// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
+int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
+    int active = 1;
+    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
+    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
+        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
+        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
+    }
+    return active;
+}
+
+// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
+// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
+    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
+    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
+    world.materials = {
+        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
+        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
+        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
+    };
+
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
+    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
+
+    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+
+    const size_t local_elements =
+        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
+    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
+    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
+
+    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
+}
+
+inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
+                                  const int global_col) {
+    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
+        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
+        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
+}
+
+inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
+                         const val_t other_energy) {
+    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
+}
+
+inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
+                          const int local_col) {
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
+    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
+    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
+    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
+    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
+
+    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
+
+    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
+    // original implementation.
+    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
+                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
+                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_row > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
+                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
+                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
+                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
+                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+
+    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
+    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
+}

-// Compute energy flux between two elements
-inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
-                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
-    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
-           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
+void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
+    request_count = 0;
+    const int rows = world.local_rows;
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+
+    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
+        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
+    }
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
+                  &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
 }

+
+// Collect a distributed field in global row-major order.  This is used only
+// for optional validation and result printing; the normal benchmark path does
+// not replicate the mesh on rank zero.
+void gatherGlobalState(const World& world, const int rank, const int process_count,
+                       const bool active, const int n_elems_root,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_energy,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_total_flux) {
+    const int local_count = active ? static_cast<int>(world.energy.size()) : 0;
+    const int local_metadata[4] = {
+        active ? world.row_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.col_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.local_rows : 0,
+        active ? world.local_cols : 0,
+    };
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<int> metadata;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(process_count);
+        displacements.resize(process_count);
+        metadata.resize(static_cast<size_t>(process_count) * 4);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&local_count, 1, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gather(local_metadata, 4, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? metadata.data() : nullptr, 4, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int gathered_count = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            displacements[process] = gathered_count;
+            gathered_count += counts[process];
+        }
     }
+
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_energy;
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_flux;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        gathered_energy.resize(gathered_count);
+        gathered_flux.resize(gathered_count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.energy.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.total_flux.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_flux.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t global_count = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
+    global_energy.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    global_total_flux.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+        const int row_begin = metadata[4 * process];
+        const int col_begin = metadata[4 * process + 1];
+        const int rows = metadata[4 * process + 2];
+        const int cols = metadata[4 * process + 3];
+        const int source_begin = displacements[process];
+        for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            const size_t destination =
+                static_cast<size_t>(row_begin + row) * n_elems_root + col_begin;
+            const size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(source_begin) +
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols;
+            std::memcpy(global_energy.data() + destination, gathered_energy.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+            std::memcpy(global_total_flux.data() + destination, gathered_flux.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+        }
+    }
 }

+constexpr int TAG_TOP = 100;
+constexpr int TAG_BOTTOM = 101;
+constexpr int TAG_LEFT = 102;

+int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    const int remainder = total % blocks;
+    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
+}
+
+int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
+// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
+// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
+int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
+    int active = 1;
+    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
+    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
+        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
+        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
+    }
+    return active;
+}
+
+// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
+// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
+    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
+    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
+    world.materials = {
+        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
+        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
+        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
+    };
+
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
+    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
+
+    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+
+    const size_t local_elements =
+        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
+    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
+    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
+
+    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
+}
+
+inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
+                                  const int global_col) {
+    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
+        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
+        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
+}
+
+inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
+                         const val_t other_energy) {
+    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
+}
+
+inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
+                          const int local_col) {
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
+    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
+    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
+    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
+    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
+
+    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
+
+    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
+    // original implementation.
+    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
+                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
+                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_row > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
+                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
+                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
+                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
+                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+
+    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
+    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
+}

+void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
+    request_count = 0;
+    const int rows = world.local_rows;
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+
+    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
+        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
+    }
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
+                  &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
 }

+// Collect a distributed field in global row-major order.  This is used only
+// for optional validation and result printing; the normal benchmark path does
+// not replicate the mesh on rank zero.
+void gatherGlobalState(const World& world, const int rank, const int process_count,
+                       const bool active, const int n_elems_root,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_energy,
+                       std::vector<val_t>& global_total_flux) {
+    const int local_count = active ? static_cast<int>(world.energy.size()) : 0;
+    const int local_metadata[4] = {
+        active ? world.row_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.col_begin : 0,
+        active ? world.local_rows : 0,
+        active ? world.local_cols : 0,
+    };
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<int> metadata;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(process_count);
+        displacements.resize(process_count);
+        metadata.resize(static_cast<size_t>(process_count) * 4);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&local_count, 1, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gather(local_metadata, 4, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? metadata.data() : nullptr, 4, MPI_INT,
+               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int gathered_count = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            displacements[process] = gathered_count;
+            gathered_count += counts[process];
+        }
     }
+
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_energy;
+    std::vector<val_t> gathered_flux;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        gathered_energy.resize(gathered_count);
+        gathered_flux.resize(gathered_count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.energy.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.total_flux.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? gathered_flux.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t global_count = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
+    global_energy.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    global_total_flux.assign(global_count, 0.0);
+    for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+        const int row_begin = metadata[4 * process];
+        const int col_begin = metadata[4 * process + 1];
+        const int rows = metadata[4 * process + 2];
+        const int cols = metadata[4 * process + 3];
+        const int source_begin = displacements[process];
+        for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            const size_t destination =
+                static_cast<size_t>(row_begin + row) * n_elems_root + col_begin;
+            const size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(source_begin) +
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols;
+            std::memcpy(global_energy.data() + destination, gathered_energy.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+            std::memcpy(global_total_flux.data() + destination, gathered_flux.data() + source,
+                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-// Validate simulation results
-bool validateResults(const World& world) {
+// Validate simulation results.  The vectors are global row-major fields on
+// rank zero, so this retains the original validation arithmetic and reporting.
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& total_flux) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
     val_t flux_sum = 0.0;

    14	// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements.
    15	using idx_t = uint64_t;
    16	using val_t = double;
    17	
    18	// Material properties for energy transfer.
    19	struct Material {
    20	    val_t transfer_coeff;
    21	    val_t external_flow;
    22	};
    23	
    24	// Material type IDs.
    25	constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
    26	constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
    27	constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;
    28	
    29	// Every rank owns one rectangular part of the global mesh.  The state is kept
    30	// in structure-of-arrays form so that the north/south halo rows are contiguous
    31	// and the update loop has unit-stride accesses.
    32	struct World {
    33	    std::vector<Material> materials;
    34	    int n_elems_root = 0;
    35	    int row_begin = 0;
    36	    int col_begin = 0;
    37	    int local_rows = 0;
    38	    int local_cols = 0;
    39	
    40	    std::vector<val_t> energy;
    41	    std::vector<val_t> total_flux;
    42	    std::vector<val_t> next_energy;
    43	    std::vector<val_t> next_total_flux;
    44	
    45	    std::vector<val_t> top_halo;
    46	    std::vector<val_t> bottom_halo;
    47	    std::vector<val_t> left_halo;
    48	    std::vector<val_t> right_halo;
    49	    std::vector<val_t> left_send;
    50	    std::vector<val_t> right_send;
    51	
    52	    MPI_Comm cart_comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    53	    int north = MPI_PROC_NULL;
    54	    int south = MPI_PROC_NULL;
    55	    int west = MPI_PROC_NULL;
    56	    int east = MPI_PROC_NULL;
    57	};
    58	
    59	constexpr int TAG_TOP = 100;
    60	constexpr int TAG_BOTTOM = 101;
    61	constexpr int TAG_LEFT = 102;
    62	constexpr int TAG_RIGHT = 103;
    63	
    64	int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
    65	    const int base = total / blocks;
    66	    const int remainder = total % blocks;
    67	    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
    68	}
    69	
    70	int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
    71	    const int base = total / blocks;
    72	    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
    73	}
    74	
    75	// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
    76	// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
    77	// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
    78	int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
    79	    int active = 1;
    80	    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
    81	    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
    82	        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
    83	        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
    84	    }
    85	    return active;
    86	}
    87	
    88	// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
    89	// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
    90	void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
    91	    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
    92	    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
    93	    world.materials = {
    94	        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
    95	        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
    96	        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
    97	    };
    98	
    99	    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
   100	    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
   101	    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
   102	    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
   103	    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
   104	
   105	    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
   106	    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
   107	    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
   108	    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
   109	
   110	    const size_t local_elements =
   111	        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
   112	    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
   113	    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
   114	    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
   115	    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
   116	
   117	    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
   118	    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
   119	    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
   120	    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
   121	    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
   122	    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
   123	
   124	    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
   125	    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
   126	}
   127	
   128	inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
   129	                                  const int global_col) {
   130	    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
   131	    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
   132	        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
   133	        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
   134	    }
   135	    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
   136	        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
   137	        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
   138	    }
   139	    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
   140	}
   141	
   142	inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
   143	                         const val_t other_energy) {
   144	    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
   145	    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
   146	}
   147	
   148	inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
   149	                          const int local_col) {
   150	    const int cols = world.local_cols;
   151	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
   152	    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
   153	    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
   154	    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
   155	    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
   156	
   157	    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
   158	
   159	    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
   160	    // original implementation.
   161	    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
   162	        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
   163	                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
   164	                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
   165	        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
   166	    }
   167	    if (global_row > 0) {
   168	        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
   169	                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
   170	        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
   171	    }
   172	    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
   173	        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
   174	                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
   175	                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
   176	        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
   177	    }
   178	    if (global_col > 0) {
   179	        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
   180	                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
   181	        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
   185	    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
   186	}
   187	
   188	// Run the communication-independent interior while halos are in flight.  An
   189	// interior element is never a global boundary/corner and always has four local
   190	// neighbours, allowing a leaner hot loop.
   191	void updateInterior(World& world) {
   192	    if (world.local_rows < 3 || world.local_cols < 3) {
   193	        return;
   194	    }
   195	
   196	    const int cols = world.local_cols;
   197	    const Material& material = world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
   198	    for (int local_row = 1; local_row + 1 < world.local_rows; ++local_row) {
   199	        const size_t row_begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols;
   200	        for (int local_col = 1; local_col + 1 < world.local_cols; ++local_col) {
   201	            const size_t index = row_begin + local_col;
   202	            const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
   203	            val_t flux = material.external_flow;
   204	            flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, world.energy[index + cols]);
   205	            flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, world.energy[index - cols]);
   206	            flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, world.energy[index + 1]);
   207	            flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, world.energy[index - 1]);
   208	            world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
   209	            world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
   210	        }
   211	    }
   212	}
   213	
   214	void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
   215	    request_count = 0;
   216	    const int rows = world.local_rows;
   217	    const int cols = world.local_cols;
   218	
   219	    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   220	        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
   221	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   222	    }
   223	    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   224	        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
   225	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   226	    }
   227	    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   228	        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
   229	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   230	    }
   231	    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   232	        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
   233	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
   237	        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
   238	        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   242	        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
   243	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   244	    }
   245	    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   246	        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
   247	                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
   248	                  &requests[request_count++]);
   249	    }
   250	    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   251	        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
   252	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   253	    }
   254	    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   255	        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
   256	                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
   257	    }
   258	}
   259	
   260	// Run the simulation for n_iters iterations.
   261	void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
   262	    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   263	        MPI_Request requests[8];
   264	        int request_count = 0;
   265	        exchangeHalos(world, requests, request_count);
   266	
   267	        updateInterior(world);
   268	
   269	        if (request_count != 0) {
   270	            MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   271	        }
   272	
   273	        // Finish only the perimeter.  The interior was updated while the
   274	        // boundary values were being exchanged.
   275	        for (int local_row = 0; local_row < world.local_rows; ++local_row) {
   276	            for (int local_col = 0; local_col < world.local_cols; ++local_col) {
   277	                const bool is_interior = local_row > 0 && local_row + 1 < world.local_rows &&
   278	                                         local_col > 0 && local_col + 1 < world.local_cols;
   279	                if (!is_interior) {
   280	                    updateElement(world, local_row, local_col);
   281	                }
   282	            }
   283	        }
   284	
   285	        world.energy.swap(world.next_energy);
   286	        world.total_flux.swap(world.next_total_flux);
   287	    }
   288	}
   289	
   290	// Collect a distributed field in global row-major order.  This is used only
   291	// for optional validation and result printing; the normal benchmark path does
   292	// not replicate the mesh on rank zero.
   293	void gatherGlobalState(const World& world, const int rank, const int process_count,
   294	                       const bool active, const int n_elems_root,
   295	                       std::vector<val_t>& global_energy,
   296	                       std::vector<val_t>& global_total_flux) {
   297	    const int local_count = active ? static_cast<int>(world.energy.size()) : 0;
   298	    const int local_metadata[4] = {
   299	        active ? world.row_begin : 0,
   300	        active ? world.col_begin : 0,
   301	        active ? world.local_rows : 0,
   302	        active ? world.local_cols : 0,
   303	    };
   304	
   305	    std::vector<int> counts;
   306	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   307	    std::vector<int> metadata;
   308	    if (rank == 0) {
   309	        counts.resize(process_count);
   310	        displacements.resize(process_count);
   311	        metadata.resize(static_cast<size_t>(process_count) * 4);
   312	    }
   313	
   314	    MPI_Gather(&local_count, 1, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
   315	               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   316	    MPI_Gather(local_metadata, 4, MPI_INT, rank == 0 ? metadata.data() : nullptr, 4, MPI_INT,
   317	               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   318	
   319	    int gathered_count = 0;
   320	    if (rank == 0) {
   321	        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
   322	            displacements[process] = gathered_count;
   323	            gathered_count += counts[process];
   324	        }
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    std::vector<val_t> gathered_energy;
   328	    std::vector<val_t> gathered_flux;
   329	    if (rank == 0) {
   330	        gathered_energy.resize(gathered_count);
   331	        gathered_flux.resize(gathered_count);
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.energy.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
   335	                rank == 0 ? gathered_energy.data() : nullptr,
   336	                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   337	                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   338	    MPI_Gatherv(active ? world.total_flux.data() : nullptr, local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
   339	                rank == 0 ? gathered_flux.data() : nullptr,
   340	                rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   341	                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   342	
   343	    if (rank != 0) {
   344	        return;
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    const size_t global_count = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * n_elems_root;
   348	    global_energy.assign(global_count, 0.0);
   349	    global_total_flux.assign(global_count, 0.0);
   350	    for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
   351	        const int row_begin = metadata[4 * process];
   352	        const int col_begin = metadata[4 * process + 1];
   353	        const int rows = metadata[4 * process + 2];
   354	        const int cols = metadata[4 * process + 3];
   355	        const int source_begin = displacements[process];
   356	        for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
   357	            const size_t destination =
   358	                static_cast<size_t>(row_begin + row) * n_elems_root + col_begin;
   359	            const size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(source_begin) +
   360	                                  static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols;
   361	            std::memcpy(global_energy.data() + destination, gathered_energy.data() + source,
   362	                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
   363	            std::memcpy(global_total_flux.data() + destination, gathered_flux.data() + source,
   364	                        static_cast<size_t>(cols) * sizeof(val_t));
   365	        }
   366	    }
   367	}
   368	
   369	// Validate simulation results.  The vectors are global row-major fields on
   370	// rank zero, so this retains the original validation arithmetic and reporting.
   371	bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
   372	                     const std::vector<val_t>& total_flux) {
   373	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
   374	    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
   375	    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   376	    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   377	
   378	    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
   379	        energy_sum += energy[i];
   380	        flux_sum += total_flux[i];
   381	        energy_max = std::max(energy[i], energy_max);
   382	        energy_min = std::min(energy[i], energy_min);
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    printf("Validation results:\n");
   386	    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   387	    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   388	    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   389	
   390	    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
   391	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   392	        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   393	        return false;
   394	    }
   395	    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
   396	        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   397	    }
   398	    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   399	        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   400	        return false;
   401	    }
   402	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   403	        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   404	        return false;
   405	    }
   406	
   407	    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   408	    return true;
   409	}
   410	
   411	// Compute the original order-independent verification hash over the global
   412	// element indices, then combine the local contributions with MPI_BXOR.
   413	uint64_t computeLocalHash(const World& world) {
   414	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   415	    for (int local_row = 0; local_row < world.local_rows; ++local_row) {
   416	        const size_t local_row_begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * world.local_cols;
   417	        const uint64_t global_row_begin =
   418	            static_cast<uint64_t>(world.row_begin + local_row) * world.n_elems_root +
   419	            world.col_begin;
   420	        for (int local_col = 0; local_col < world.local_cols; ++local_col) {
   421	            const size_t local_index = local_row_begin + local_col;
   422	            uint64_t energy_bits;
   423	            uint64_t flux_bits;
   424	            std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &world.energy[local_index], sizeof(energy_bits));
   425	            std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &world.total_flux[local_index], sizeof(flux_bits));
   426	            const uint64_t global_index = global_row_begin + local_col;
   427	            hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   428	            hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
   429	        }
   430	    }
   431	    return hash;
   432	}
   433	
   434	size_t localStateBytes(const World& world) {
   435	    return (world.energy.size() + world.total_flux.size() + world.next_energy.size() +
   436	            world.next_total_flux.size() + world.top_halo.size() + world.bottom_halo.size() +
   437	            world.left_halo.size() + world.right_halo.size() + world.left_send.size() +
   438	            world.right_send.size()) *
   439	           sizeof(val_t);
   440	}
   441	
   442	void printUsage(const char* prog_name) {
   443	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", prog_name);
   444	    printf("Options:\n");
   445	    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
   446	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
   447	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   448	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   449	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   450	}
   451	
   452	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   453	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   454	
   455	    int rank = 0;
   456	    int process_count = 1;
   457	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   458	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &process_count);
   459	
   460	    int n_elems_root = 512;
   461	    int n_iters = 10;
   462	    bool validate = false;
   463	    bool print_results_enabled = false;
   464	    bool show_usage = false;
   465	    bool bad_arguments = false;
   466	
   467	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   468	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   469	            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);
   470	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   471	            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);
   472	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   473	            validate = true;
   474	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   475	            print_results_enabled = true;
   476	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   477	            show_usage = true;
   478	        } else {
   479	            if (rank == 0) {
   480	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   481	            }
   482	            bad_arguments = true;
   483	        }
   484	    }
   485	
   486	    const int max_root = 46340;  // sqrt(INT_MAX), required by MPI count arguments.
   487	    if (n_elems_root <= 0 || n_elems_root > max_root || n_iters < 0) {
   488	        if (rank == 0) {
   489	            printf("Grid size must be in [1, %d] and iterations must be non-negative.\n",
   490	                   max_root);
   491	        }
   492	        bad_arguments = true;
   493	    }
   494	    if (show_usage || bad_arguments) {
   495	        if (rank == 0) {
   496	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   497	        }
   498	        MPI_Finalize();
   499	        return bad_arguments ? 1 : 0;
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    const int active_processes = activeProcessCount(process_count, n_elems_root);
   503	    const bool active = rank < active_processes;
   504	    MPI_Comm active_comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   505	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, active ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, rank, &active_comm);
   506	
   507	    World world;
   508	    if (active) {
   509	        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
   510	        MPI_Dims_create(active_processes, 2, dims);
   511	        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
   512	        MPI_Comm cart_comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   513	        MPI_Cart_create(active_comm, 2, dims, periods, 0, &cart_comm);
   514	        MPI_Comm_free(&active_comm);
   515	        buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root, cart_comm);
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    const size_t global_elements =
   519	        static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root) * static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root);
   520	    const unsigned long long local_memory =

+constexpr int TAG_TOP = 100;
+constexpr int TAG_BOTTOM = 101;
+constexpr int TAG_LEFT = 102;
+constexpr int TAG_RIGHT = 103;

+int blockStart(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    const int remainder = total % blocks;
+    return block * base + std::min(block, remainder);
+}
+
+int blockSize(const int total, const int blocks, const int block) {
+    const int base = total / blocks;
+    return base + (block < total % blocks ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// Select the largest rectangular process grid whose dimensions do not exceed
+// the mesh dimensions.  This keeps every active rank useful, including for
+// small problem sizes or oversubscribed launches.
+int activeProcessCount(const int process_count, const int n_elems_root) {
+    int active = 1;
+    const int max_rows = std::min(process_count, n_elems_root);
+    for (int row_ranks = 1; row_ranks <= max_rows; ++row_ranks) {
+        const int col_ranks = std::min(n_elems_root, process_count / row_ranks);
+        active = std::max(active, row_ranks * col_ranks);
+    }
+    return active;
+}
+
+// Build the distributed 2-D square grid.  Connectivity is implicit: this is
+// exactly the same up/down/right/left adjacency built by the original mesh.
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, MPI_Comm cart_comm) {
+    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;
+    world.cart_comm = cart_comm;
+    world.materials = {
+        {0.8, 0.0},   // Default material
+        {0.8, 0.5},   // Inflow material
+        {0.8, -0.5},  // Outflow material
+    };
+
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Cart_coords(cart_comm, 0, 2, coords);
+    MPI_Cart_get(cart_comm, 2, dims, periods, coords);
+
+    world.row_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.col_begin = blockStart(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    world.local_rows = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[0], coords[0]);
+    world.local_cols = blockSize(n_elems_root, dims[1], coords[1]);
+
+    const size_t local_elements =
+        static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * static_cast<size_t>(world.local_cols);
+    world.energy.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.total_flux.assign(local_elements, 0.0);
+    world.next_energy.resize(local_elements);
+    world.next_total_flux.resize(local_elements);
+
+    world.top_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.bottom_halo.resize(world.local_cols);
+    world.left_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_halo.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.left_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+    world.right_send.resize(world.local_rows);
+
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 0, 1, &world.north, &world.south);
+    MPI_Cart_shift(cart_comm, 1, 1, &world.west, &world.east);
+}
+
+inline const Material& materialAt(const World& world, const int global_row,
+                                  const int global_col) {
+    const int last = world.n_elems_root - 1;
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == 0) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == last)) {
+        return world.materials[INFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    if ((global_row == 0 && global_col == last) ||
+        (global_row == last && global_col == 0)) {
+        return world.materials[OUTFLOW_MAT_ID];
+    }
+    return world.materials[DEFAULT_MAT_ID];
+}
+
+inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& material, const val_t this_energy,
+                         const val_t other_energy) {
+    // Keep the arithmetic order used by the original computeFlux function.
+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * material.transfer_coeff * 1.0 * 0.25;
+}
+
+inline void updateElement(World& world, const int local_row,
+                          const int local_col) {
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * cols + local_col;
+    const int global_row = world.row_begin + local_row;
+    const int global_col = world.col_begin + local_col;
+    const val_t current_energy = world.energy[index];
+    const Material& material = materialAt(world, global_row, global_col);
+
+    val_t flux = material.external_flow;
+
+    // The order is down, up, right, left, matching buildSquare2D in the
+    // original implementation.
+    if (global_row + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < world.local_rows
+                                   ? world.energy[index + cols]
+                                   : world.bottom_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_row > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? world.energy[index - cols]
+                                              : world.top_halo[local_col];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col + 1 < world.n_elems_root) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col + 1 < world.local_cols
+                                   ? world.energy[index + 1]
+                                   : world.right_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+    if (global_col > 0) {
+        const val_t neighbor = local_col > 0 ? world.energy[index - 1]
+                                              : world.left_halo[local_row];
+        flux += computeFlux(material, current_energy, neighbor);
+    }
+
+    world.next_energy[index] = current_energy + flux;
+    world.next_total_flux[index] = world.total_flux[index] + std::abs(flux);
+}

-// Compute energy flux between two elements
-inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
-                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
-    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
-           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
+void exchangeHalos(World& world, MPI_Request* requests, int& request_count) {
+    request_count = 0;
+    const int rows = world.local_rows;
+    const int cols = world.local_cols;
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.top_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_TOP,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.bottom_halo.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.left_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(world.right_halo.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+
+    for (int row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        world.left_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols];
+        world.right_send[row] = world.energy[static_cast<size_t>(row) * cols + cols - 1];
+    }
+
+    if (world.north != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), cols, MPI_DOUBLE, world.north, TAG_BOTTOM,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.south != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + static_cast<size_t>(rows - 1) * cols, cols,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, world.south, TAG_TOP, world.cart_comm,
+                  &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.west != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.left_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.west, TAG_RIGHT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
+    if (world.east != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Isend(world.right_send.data(), rows, MPI_DOUBLE, world.east, TAG_LEFT,
+                  world.cart_comm, &requests[request_count++]);
+    }
 }
